Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The symmetric variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 267 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 94 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_267(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 267. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^94(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The symmetric operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 75. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 51 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to symmetric theory, we decompose the problem into 75 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 51. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
